**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/compare-spatial-distributions.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/compare-spatial-distributions.html

**text**　輸入源和目標資料集必須是特性或兩者兼有,只支持點和多邊形特征。多邊形在分析前將轉換為機器人點。如果源和目標特征是不同的預測,目標特征將投射到源特征。對于弧星體,源和目標激光器必須具有相同的單元格大小、范圍和協調系統,以便源光柵的每個單元格都與目標光柵的單個單元格一致。盡管EMD與重新分配和資源分布密切相關,但也可以用于模式比較。重新定位和資源分配工作流程通常應使用網絡分析擴展,而且這一工具應用來比較空間模式。比較適用性或綜合指數表面——編制適當性和綜合指數表面需要做出選擇,例如使用哪些變量、如何加權變量、以及是否使用變量或調整尺度。 EMD可用于比較使用不同選項和變量的由此得出的表面,這樣可以量化適合性或指數值對這些選擇的敏感性,通常比相關系數等逐個地點比較更為可取。 EMD也可以用來比較不同現象或變量的適宜性和指數表層,例如將社會脆弱性指數與熱暴露指數進行比較。評估時間變化——通過提供按來源和目標兩個時間步驟收集的相同變量,EMD可以量化事件、客戶或野生動物觀測的空間分布在時間間隔期間的變化。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "The input source and target datasets must both be features or both be rasters. Only point and polygon features are supported. Polygons will be converted to centroid points prior to analysis. If the source and target features are in different projections, the target features will be projected to the source features. For rasters, the source and target rasters must have the same cell size, extent, and coordinate system so that every cell of the source raster aligns with a single cell of the target raster.\nWhile EMD is closely related to reallocation and resource distribution, it is also used for pattern comparison. Reallocation and resource distribution workflows should usually use the Network Analyst extension, and this tool should be used to compare spatial patterns.\nPossible applications of the tool include the following: Comparing suitability or composite index surfaces—Creating suitability and composite index surfaces requires making choices such as which variables to use, how to weight the variables, and whether to use transformations or rescalings. EMD can be used to compare resulting surfaces that used different options and variables. This allows you to quantify the sensitivity of the suitability or index values to these choices, and it is generally preferable to a location-by-location comparison such as a correlation coefficient. EMD can also be used to compare suitability and index surfaces made for different phenomena or variables, such as comparing social vulnerability indices to heat exposure indices. Comparing density or interpolation surfaces—EMD can compare density surfaces or compare the results of different interpolation methods. Evaluating changes over time—By providing the same variable collected in two time steps as the source and target, EMD can quantify how the spatial distribution of incidents, customers, or wildlife observations has changed between the time periods.", "section": "Usage"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/compare-spatial-distributions.html#usage

## 比較空間分布(地球移動距離)(空間統計工具) · 使用率

**id**　021c71a992ca68f846-4

**english**　Compare Spatial Distributions (Earth Mover Distance)

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 空間統計工具箱
- 分析模式工具et

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/compare-spatial-distributions.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/compare-spatial-distributions.html

**text**　對于特性,可以提供分析欄位以顯示源和目標特征的質量值。至少一個數值必須在每個欄位中大于零,并且所有負值都將在分析前被篩選。如果不提供欄位,則對每個特性將指定相等質量。欄位的數值將標準化為分析前的一個數值的總和。要比較同一特性類中兩個欄位的空間模式,提供源和目標特征的特性類別,并提供作為分析欄位的特性類別。對于光標,光標的VALUE欄位將用作源群和目標激光器。這些數值將正常化為分析前的一個數值。因為分析欄位或分析欄位。光柵值各歸一個,工具在比較空間模式時不考慮規模。例如,假設您正在比較無家可歸地點的空間分布和隨后幾年在某個縣之間的計數。如果總體無家可歸者比率上升5%,但全縣的增幅是相同的,沒有集中在任何特定地區,環境監測中心將表明該縣的格局大致相同;它不會確定無家可歸者人口數量的總體增長情況。分析領域或光柵細胞必須標準化為一,不能使用負值,因為EMD是概率分布的對比,概率分布必須是一次總和,不能是負的。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "For features, analysis fields can be provided indicating the mass values of the source and target features. At least one value must be greater than zero in each field, and all negative values will be filtered prior to analysis. If no field is provided, an equal mass will be assigned to each feature. The values of the fields will be normalized to sum to one prior to analysis. To compare the spatial patterns of two fields from the same feature class, provide the feature class as both the source and target features and provide the fields as the analysis fields. For rasters, the VALUE field of the rasters will be used as the masses of the source and target rasters. The values will be normalized to sum to one prior to analysis.\nBecause the analysis fields or raster values are each normalized to sum to one, the tool does not take magnitude into account when comparing spatial patterns. For example, suppose you are comparing the spatial distributions of homeless locations and counts across a county in subsequent years. If the overall homeless rate increases by five percent, but the increase is uniform across the county and not concentrated in any particular area, the EMD will indicate that the patterns in the county are largely the same. It will not identify the overall increase in the magnitude of the homeless population. The analysis fields or raster cells must be normalized to sum to one and cannot use negative values because EMD is a comparison of probability distributions, and probability distributions must sum to one and cannot be negative.", "section": "Usage"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/compare-spatial-distributions.html#usage

## 比較空間分布(地球移動距離)(空間統計工具) · 使用率

**id**　021c71a992ca68f846-5

**english**　Compare Spatial Distributions (Earth Mover Distance)

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 空間統計工具箱
- 分析模式工具et

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/compare-spatial-distributions.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/compare-spatial-distributions.html

**text**　該工具返回 EMD 值和作為衍生輸出的正常 EMD 值, 并打印在信件中。 EMD 是源特性或光柵的協調系統單位, 被解釋為來源特性或光柵的最大對稱距離的平均距離( 按正常質量加權) , 源值必須移動到目標方, 以使值相同。 正常 EMD 值的計算方法是將 EMD 值除以最大可能的值, 轉換為百分比, 以便消除空間尺度和范圍的影響。 例如, 5% 的標準化值意味著平均數值必須在源特性或光柵單元格與目標特性或光網格之間移動5%的最大對對角距離。 對于特性, 則計算成一個普通值。工具, 工具創建了源特性的特征類和目標特性的特征類。 特性類包括平均距離( 按正常質量加權) 欄位, 數值移入或調出該特性、 特性的正常質量以及進出該特性的平均費用。 特性層將使用一個雙變量化的正常質量和平均距離的顏色方案來繪制。 大質量和大距離的特征將具有最高的成本, 雙軌制使您能夠看到高成本是因具有大質量、 大距離或兩者兼而有之特征造成的。 如果不提供分析欄位, 所有正常質量值都將相等, 因此這個層次將取出相對于特性的平均成本。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "The tool returns the EMD value and a normalized EMD value as derived outputs and are also printed in the messages. The EMD is in the units of the coordinate system of the source features or raster and is interpreted as the average distance (weighted by normalized mass) that the values of the source must move to the target to make the values the same. The normalized EMD value is calculated by dividing the EMD by its maximum possible value and converting to a percent in order to remove the effect of spatial scale and extent. For example, a normalized value of 5 percent means that on average, the values must move 5 percent of the largest pairwise distance between a source feature or raster cell and a target feature or raster cell.\nFor features, the tool creates a feature class of the source features and a feature class of the target features as output. The feature classes include fields of the average distance (weighted by normalized mass) that the values moved to or from the feature, the normalized mass of the feature, and the average cost of the movement to or from the feature. The feature layers will draw using a bivariate color scheme of the normalized mass and average distance. Features with large masses and large distances will have the highest costs, and the bivariate scheme allows you to see whether the high cost is due to the feature having a large mass, a large distance, or both. If no analysis field is provided, all normalized mass values will be equal, so the layer will instead draw based on the average cost of the feature.", "section": "Usage"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/compare-spatial-distributions.html#usage

## 比較空間分布(地球移動距離)(空間統計工具) · 使用率

**id**　021c71a992ca68f846-6

**english**　Compare Spatial Distributions (Earth Mover Distance)

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 空間統計工具箱
- 分析模式工具et

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/compare-spatial-distributions.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/compare-spatial-distributions.html

**text**　對于 RARTER , 該工具創建了兩個多波段源和目標弧, 帶平均成本、 平均距離和每個單元格的普通質量的波段。 默認情況下將顯示成本波段, 但其他波段可以使用符號窗體顯示 。 對于 RATOS , GPU 處理可用于提高性能。 這對于擁有100萬多個單元格的 RATER 來說最為重要。 如果 PyToch Python 模塊安裝在兼容的 GPU 中, 它將被默認地使用。 您可以使用 Proctor 類型環境 在 CPU 和 GPU 處理和控制之間選擇哪些 GPU 用于 GPU ID 環境。 要安裝 PyTorch 。或者,打開 Python 命令窗口并鍵入以下命令,然后按 Enter 鍵: Conda 安裝 pytorch 對于在源或目標特性中具有大約10,000個特征的輸入特性,建議用點到角工具將這些特性轉換成激光儀。對于多邊形,在將其轉換為光柵之前使用功能到點工具將多邊形轉換成點。使用范圍、單元格大小和輸出座標系統環境以確保兩個光標具有相同的細胞大小、范圍和協調系統。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "For rasters, the tool creates two multiband rasters of the source and target rasters with bands of the average cost, average distance, and normalized mass of each cell. The cost band will be displayed by default, but the other bands can be displayed using the Symbology pane.\nFor rasters, GPU processing can be used to increase performance. This will be most significant for rasters with more than 1 million cells. If the PyTorch Python module is installed with a compatible GPU, it will be used by default. You can use the Processor Type environment to select between CPU and GPU processing and control which GPU will be used with the GPU ID environment. To install the PyTorch module, install the deep learning frameworks. Alternatively, open the Python Command Window and type the following command, then press Enter: conda install pytorch\nFor input features with more than approximately 10,000 features in the source or target features, it is recommended to convert the features to rasters with the Point To Raster tool. For polygons, convert the polygons to point centroids using the Feature To Point tool prior to converting them to raster. Use the Extent, Cell Size, and Output Coordinate System environments to ensure that both rasters have the same cell size, extent, and coordinate system.", "section": "Usage"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/compare-spatial-distributions.html#usage

## 比較空間分布(地球移動距離)(空間統計工具) · 使用率

**id**　021c71a992ca68f846-7

**english**　Compare Spatial Distributions (Earth Mover Distance)

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 空間統計工具箱
- 分析模式工具et

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/compare-spatial-distributions.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/compare-spatial-distributions.html

**text**　對于特性,使用 POT Python 軟件包來計算精確的 EMD 值。對于 RARTS , EMD 的 Sinkhorn 近似值被用來有效計算含有多個單元格的 raster 的結果。 這一近似值要求源和目標資料集的數值在矩形網格中對齊,因此它自然適合 RATS , 而準確的 EMD 允許源和目標特征具有任何空間配置, 不需要對齊 。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "For features, an exact EMD value is calculated using the POT Python package. For rasters, a Sinkhorn approximation of the EMD is used to efficiently calculate results for rasters with many cells. This approximation requires that the values of the source and target datasets align in a rectangular grid, so it is naturally suited to rasters, whereas the exact EMD allows the source and target features to have any spatial configuration and do not need to align.", "section": "Usage"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/compare-spatial-distributions.html#usage

## 比較空間分布(地球移動距離)(空間統計工具) · 衍生輸出

**id**　021c71a992ca68f846-16

**english**　Compare Spatial Distributions (Earth Mover Distance)

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 空間統計工具箱
- 分析模式工具et

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/compare-spatial-distributions.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/compare-spatial-distributions.html

**parameter**　normalized_emd

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 標簽標簽
- 解釋解釋解釋解釋
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 地球移動器距離
- 源與目標特性或光柵之間的正常地移動器距離值。 該值是通過將地球移動器距離除以其最大可能值并轉換為百分比來計算的。 例如, 12 值意味著在源和目標特征或光柵之間,地球移動器距離是最大可能的地面移動器距離的12%。
- 雙雙

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"values": ["Normalized Earth Mover Distance", "The normalized earth mover distance value between the source and target features or raster. The value is calculated by dividing the earth mover distance by its maximum possible value and converting to a percent. For example, the value 12 means that the earth mover distance is 12 percent of the maximum possible earth mover distance between the locations of the source and target features or raster..", "Double"], "section": "Derived output"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/compare-spatial-distributions.html#derived-output

**text**　# Calculate earth mover distance between two feature layers.
arcpy.env.workspace = r"c:\data\project_data.gdb"
arcpy.stats.CompareSpatialDistributions(
    in_source=r"mySourcFeatures",
    in_target=r"myTargetFeatures",
    out_source_dataset=r"myOutputSourceFeatures",
    out_target_dataset=r"myOutputTargetFeatures",
    source_analysis_field="sourceMass",
    target_analysis_field="targetMass"
)

import arcpy

# Set the current workspace.
arcpy.env.workspace = r"c:\data\project_data.gdb"

# Run the tool.

    in_source=r"mySourcRaster",

)

For features, the values will be stored in fields of the output features, and for rasters, a three band raster will be created with bands for average distance, average cost, and normalized mass."}, {"parameter": "out_target_dataset", "label_zh": "輸出目標特性或光柵", "label_en": "Output Target Features or Raster", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Class, Raster Dataset", "description_en": "The output features or raster containing the mean distance, mean cost, and normalized mass of each target feature or raster cell. ## 比較空間分布(地球移動距離)(空間統計工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 空間統計工具箱
- 分析模式工具et

**english**　Compare Spatial Distributions (Earth Mover Distance)

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/compare-spatial-distributions.html

**id**　021c71a992ca68f846-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/compare-spatial-distributions.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Compare Spatial Distributions (Earth Mover Distance)，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入源源特性或光柵 [in_source]、輸入目標特性或光柵 [in_target]、輸出源源特性或光柵 [out_source_dataset]、輸出目標特性或光柵 [out_target_dataset]、來源分析欄位(備選) [source_analysis_field]、目標分析實地(備選) [target_analysis_field]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

## 添加遵循角度布局 (網絡邏輯示意圖工具) · 匯總

**id**　02381c5bad2ecc6bb1-1

**english**　Add Angle Directed Layout

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 網絡邏輯示意圖工具箱
- 配置工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-diagram/add-angle-directed-layout.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-diagram/add-angle-directed-layout.html

**text**　用于將遵循角度布局算法添加到基于給定模板生成邏輯示意圖結束時自動進行鏈接的布局列表。 此工具還會針對基于該模板的任意邏輯示意圖預設遵循角度布局算法參數。
此布局算法可沿指定對齊方向逐步移動邏輯示意圖邊。 對于每條邏輯示意圖邊，該布局會注釋其當前方向，然后在指定的各個方向中搜索最近的方向，然后沿該方向對邊進行移動。
了解有關遵循角度布局算法的詳細信息

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/network-diagram/add-angle-directed-layout.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-diagram/add-angle-directed-layout.html

**text**　系統在使用公共設施網絡或追蹤網絡服務時不支持此工具。 您必須使用文件或移動地理資料庫中的公共設施網絡或追蹤網絡，或者企業級地理資料庫中公共設施網絡或追蹤網絡的資料庫連接。 當使用企業級地理資料庫時，需要滿足以下要求： 輸入網絡參數值必須來自作為資料庫公共設施網絡所有者或資料庫追蹤網絡所有者建立的資料庫連接。 連接的 ArcGIS Enterprise 門戶賬戶必須為門戶公共設施網絡所有者或門戶追蹤網絡所有者。 該工具必須連接到預期為公開且不受保護的默認版本。
要基于邏輯示意圖模板對邏輯示意圖預設布局算法參數，請取消選中活動參數（Python 中 is_active = &quot;INACTIVE&quot;）。
如果您想要將布局算法添加到基于輸入邏輯示意圖模板生成邏輯示意圖結束時自動進行鏈接的布局列表，請在活動處于選中狀態（Python 中 is_active = &quot;ACTIVE&quot;）的情況下運行此工具。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/network-diagram/add-angle-directed-layout.html#usage

**id**　02381c5bad2ecc6bb1-8

**english**　Add Angle Directed Layout

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 網絡邏輯示意圖工具箱
- 配置工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-diagram/add-angle-directed-layout.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-diagram/add-angle-directed-layout.html

**parameter**　is_active

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 活動
- 指定是否將在基于指定模板生成邏輯示意圖時自動運行布局算法。 選中—添加的布局算法會在基于輸入模板生成任何邏輯示意圖的過程中自動運行。 這是默認設置。 為布局算法指定的參數值是在生成邏輯示意圖的過程中用于運行布局的參數值。 如果要對基于輸入模板的任何邏輯示意圖運行此算法，則還會默認加載這些參數值。 未選中—如果要對基于輸入模板的任何邏輯示意圖運行此算法，則還將默認加載當前為添加的布局邏輯示意圖指定的所有參數值。
- Boolean

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/network-diagram/add-angle-directed-layout.html#parameters

## 添加遵循角度布局 (網絡邏輯示意圖工具) · 參數

**id**　02381c5bad2ecc6bb1-11

**english**　Add Angle Directed Layout

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 網絡邏輯示意圖工具箱
- 配置工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-diagram/add-angle-directed-layout.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-diagram/add-angle-directed-layout.html

**parameter**　number_of_directions

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 方向數 （可選）
- 對齊邏輯示意圖邊及其連接的交匯點將使用的方向數。 12 個方向—將移動邊，以使其逐漸接近 12 個軸中的一個（軸從邊的起始交匯點處開始，傾斜角度分別為 30 度、60 度、90 度、120 度、150 度、180 度、210 度、240 度、270 度、300 度、330 度或 360 度）。 8 個方向—將移動邊，以使其逐漸接近 8 個軸中的一個（軸從邊的起始交匯點處開始，傾斜角度分別為 45 度、90 度、135 度、180 度、225 度、270 度、315 度或 360 度）。 這是默認設置。 4 個方向—將移動邊，以使其逐漸接近 4 個軸中的一個（軸從邊的起始交匯點處開始，傾斜角度分別為 90 度、180 度、270 度或 360 度）。
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/network-diagram/add-angle-directed-layout.html#parameters
